# Q-18 RECONCILIATION -- the rebuilt era vs CONTEXT 4.6's sealed numbers

Generated by `docs/recovery/q18_reconcile.py` (implementation:
`src/acumen/recovery_reconcile.py`), committed under CLAUDE.md's evidence rule.
Offline and read-only. Regenerate with:

    python docs/recovery/q18_reconcile.py --out docs/recovery/q18_reconciliation.md

The architect's ruling this executes (QUESTIONS.md, 31-Jul-2026, verbatim):

> the rebuilt era MUST be reconciled against CONTEXT 4.6's sealed numbers; every
> divergence classified {new-CA-explained, vendor-repair-explained, unexplained};
> explained drift is accepted only via a formal CONTEXT 4.6 amendment (v1.5) listing
> exact deltas; unexplained drift is a defect to triage.

## 0. Inputs

| Side | Source | Scope end | Symbols |
|---|---|---|---|
| SEALED | `docs\backfill_minute_report.md` | 2026-07-28 | 210 |
| REBUILT | `docs\recovery\backfill_minute_report_rebuild.md` | 2026-07-31 | 210 |

The sealed side is the COMMITTED report -- the artefact CONTEXT 4.6 summarises, and the one thing the incident could not touch. Both sides are read by the same parser. 41,372 corporate-action row(s) were read from the rebuilt day-cache, offline.

## 1. The classification rules this report applied

The ruling names the three classes; these are the tests, printed so they can be
overruled by reading. A divergence is called explained only when the evidence is on
disk -- the default is `unexplained`.

| Class | Applies to | Requires |
|---|---|---|
| `new-CA-explained` | gate outcomes only (gate-1 effective passes, status) | an ex-date AFTER the sealed era in the rebuilt CA cache. A corporate action re-scales a back-adjusted history; it never adds or removes stored days |
| `vendor-repair-explained` | improvements only | the SEALED era named this symbol's deficiency (quarantined, gate-1P residual table, or gate-3 disclosed-residual register) |
| `sealed-fetch-horizon` | stored days, and the gate-1 pass count when it MATCHES | T1: a boundary date B inside the sealed fetch window whose running count equals the sealed count exactly, AND the whole gate-1 move reproduced by re-running CONTEXT 4.5's gates over the days after B |
| `vendor-snapshot-drift` | whatever the T3 forensics named, per symbol | T3: a committed, era-keyed forensics verdict at `docs/recovery/q18_t3_forensics.json`. Never inferred here; a symbol the forensics ESCALATED keeps its `unexplained` class |
| `unexplained` | everything else | -- including every regression, every one-sided presence, and every change on a symbol with no disclosed deficiency |

T2 (architect, 01-Aug-2026) tightened `new-CA-explained`: an ex-date must fall in (sealed scope end, 2026-07-31] -- after the sealed era AND on or before the rebuild's own fetch date. 85 corporate-action row(s) with a FUTURE ex-date were dropped by that tightening; they explain nothing about a history the vendor had already served.

## 4a. T1 -- the SEALED-FETCH-HORIZON boundary test

The architect's T1 (QUESTIONS.md, 01-Aug-2026). For each symbol, B is the rebuilt store's `SEALED days`-th stored date, so the store's count on dates <= B equals the sealed count exactly and B is unique. The test PASSES when B falls inside the sealed fetch window 2026-07-20..2026-07-28.

The gate-count leg is MEASURED, and it is a two-sided bound rather than an equality, because the sealed era's per-day verdicts no longer exist to be differenced. CONTEXT 4.5's own battery is re-run here over the rebuilt store's days in the fetch window [2026-07-20, 2026-07-31]; the sealed era's own tail in that window is known exactly (it is the store's day count over [window start, B]); and what that sealed tail could have contributed is bounded by the sealed report's own totals -- its ungated days (the CONTEXT 4.6 store-lag) and its failures are the only slack there is. If the reported gate-1 move lands outside the resulting interval, a day OLDER than the fetch window changed verdict, no horizon reading explains it, and the symbol keeps `unexplained` on that measure. What this proves is attribution to the window, not the identity of individual days.

- symbols tested: **210**
- boundary inside the sealed fetch window (T1 PASS): **210**
- of those, the gate-1 move is attributable to the fetch window: **204**
- gate-1 move NOT attributable to the window (these fall through to the other classes): **6**: APLAPOLLO, BSE, GAIL, LODHA, NESTLEIND, POWERGRID
- T1 FAIL (stays unexplained): **0**

### Boundary-date histogram

| Boundary date B | Symbols | Extra days inside the sealed scope |
|---|---|---|
| 2026-07-24 | 37 | 2 |
| 2026-07-27 | 168 | 1 |
| 2026-07-28 | 5 | 0 |

A histogram concentrated on a handful of consecutive dates inside the sealed fetch window is the signature the ruling names: the sealed run fetched each symbol at a different point of a long night, so its per-symbol horizon was earlier than the single scope date its report is labelled with.

## 6. Independent leg -- does the rebuilt report describe the store on disk?

Each symbol's stored days counted straight from the minute store's parquet files over the rebuilt report's own scope, and compared with that report's section 3. A CONTRADICTION (the report claims depth, the parquet directory holds nothing) blocks the verdict; a plain count difference is printed as a note, because the report's `Days` column counts from each symbol's own clamp and this module will not fail a rebuild over a semantic it cannot verify.

AGREES on all 210 rebuilt symbols. The report is a faithful description of the store this reconciliation read.

## 7. Growth OUTSIDE the sealed era (not a divergence)

630 stored symbol-day(s) fall after the sealed scope end (2026-07-28). They are new trading, not drift, and they are what extends the walkable span past the sealed era.

Note precisely what this does and does not remove from the comparison. Sections 4 and 5 diff the two reports' OWN columns, and the rebuilt report's `Days` counts to its own scope end (2026-07-31) -- so these 630 days ARE inside its figures. Nothing is quietly netted off here; instead section 4a's boundary test is what separates the two causes, by naming the exact dates on each side of B.

## 8. VERDICT

**ZERO UNEXPLAINED DIVERGENCES.** Under the ruling, the explained drift above is accepted ONLY via a formal CONTEXT 4.6 amendment (v1.5) listing the exact deltas. Section 2's delta column and section 5's table ARE that payload; writing the amendment is the architect's, not a session's.

Divergences by FINAL class:

| Class | Count |
|---|---|
| `new-CA-explained` | 0 |
| `vendor-repair-explained` | 3 |
| `sealed-fetch-horizon` | 414 |
| `vendor-snapshot-drift` | 5 |
| `unexplained` | 0 |
